3.3.3AdvantagesofNaiveBayesClassifier
NaiveBayesclassifierhasthefollowingadvantages.
1.It can handlemissing data attributevalues.
2.Itcan handle irrelevant input variables.
3.Itissimpletoimplementwithoutrequiringspecialsoftwarelibraries.
4.It iscomputationally fast.
5.Itperformsbetterwithcategorical variableswhen compared withdecision trees.

Thespectral clustering family can beviewed asa.three-stepalgorithm.
(1)Thefirst stepistoconstruct a similarity graphforallthe data points.
(2)The data points are embedded ina space,inwhich the clusters aremoreobvious,with the use of the

The low-dimensional representation obtained in the second step isalsoreferred toasspectral embeddingand
has applicationsbeyond the clustering contextsuch as dimensionalityreduction.Theword spectral is used to
denote thefact that the clusteringresultsareobtained by analysing thespectrum of thegraphLaplacian.

6.3.2（A)BellmanEquation
TheBemanequationisafundamental conceptindnamicprogrammingandreinforcementlearning.tdescribes
therelationshipbetweenthevalueofastateorstate-actionpairandthevaluesofitsneighbouringstates.
Insimpleterms,theBellmanequationstatesthatthevalueofbeinginaparticularstate（ortakingaspecificaction
inastate）isequaltotheimmediaterewardobtainedfromthatstate（orstate-actionpair)plustheexpectedvalue
Mathematically,theBellmanequationcanbewrittenasfollows:
V(s)=R(s）+y*∑[P(s,a,s)*V(s)]
